ac_release_snapshot_world = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_registry_ready } }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = AWW }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_adopted
			AWW = { set_country_flag = ac_release_adopted_existing }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_transfer_done
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_entry_begun
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_legacy_oob_done
			else = {
				set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_was_absent
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = REC }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_adopted
			REC = { set_country_flag = ac_release_adopted_existing }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_legacy_oob_done
			else = {
				set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_was_absent
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = REL }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_adopted
			REL = { set_country_flag = ac_release_adopted_existing }
			else = {
				set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_was_absent
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = SBK }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_adopted
			SBK = { set_country_flag = ac_release_adopted_existing }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_transfer_done
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_entry_begun
			else = {
				set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_was_absent
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = SER }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_adopted
			SER = { set_country_flag = ac_release_adopted_existing }
			else = {
				set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_was_absent
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = SHI }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_adopted
			SHI = { set_country_flag = ac_release_adopted_existing }
			else = {
				set_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_was_absent
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = TER }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_TER_adopted
			TER = { set_country_flag = ac_release_adopted_existing }
			else = {
				set_global_flag = ac_release_TER_was_absent
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_registry_ready
	}
}
ac_release_AWW_select_owned_capital = {

			owns_state = 224

		set_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected
	}
	else_if = {

			owns_state = 272

ac_release_REC_select_owned_capital = {

		limit = {
			owns_state = 345
			controls_province = 7826
		}
		set_capital = { state = 345 remember_old_capital = no }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			owns_state = 300 owns_state = 316 owns_state = 345
			all_owned_state = { OR = { state = 300 state = 316 state = 345 } }
			controls_province = 7826
		}
		set_capital = { state = 345 remember_old_capital = no }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected
	}

ac_release_SHI_select_owned_capital = {
	clr_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected
	if = {
		limit = {
			owns_state = 426
			controls_province = 2077
		}
		set_capital = { state = 426 remember_old_capital = no }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			owns_state = 426 owns_state = 447 owns_state = 607
			all_owned_state = { OR = { state = 426 state = 447 state = 607 } }
			controls_province = 2077
		}
		set_capital = { state = 426 remember_old_capital = no }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			owns_state = 116
			controls_province = 5860
		}
		set_capital = { state = 116 remember_old_capital = no }
		set_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected

ac_release_observe_country = {
	ac_release_snapshot_world = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = AWW original_tag = AWW any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_administration_registered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_adopted } has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_was_absent NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_country_flag = ac_release_awaiting_legacy_army }
		}
		ac_release_AWW_select_owned_capital = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
			ac_econ_adopt_release_account = yes
			ac_pol_initialize = yes
			ac_release_AWW_physical_registration = yes
			set_country_flag = ac_release_administration_registered
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_administration_registered
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_legacy_oob_done
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_transfer_done
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_entry_begun
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = no }
		ac_release_AWW_select_owned_capital = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_adopted ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
		ac_econ_adopt_release_account = yes
		ac_pol_initialize = yes
		set_country_flag = ac_release_administration_registered
		set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_administration_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } has_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_maintenance_registered }
		ac_release_AWW_prepare_maintenance = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = REC original_tag = REC any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_administration_registered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_adopted } has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_was_absent NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		ac_release_REC_select_owned_capital = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
			ac_econ_adopt_release_account = yes
			ac_pol_initialize = yes
			ac_release_REC_physical_registration = yes
			set_country_flag = ac_release_administration_registered
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_administration_registered
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_legacy_oob_done
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = no }
		ac_release_REC_select_owned_capital = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_adopted ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
		ac_econ_adopt_release_account = yes
		ac_pol_initialize = yes
		set_country_flag = ac_release_administration_registered
		set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_administration_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } has_global_flag = ac_release_REC_maintenance_registered }
		ac_release_REC_prepare_maintenance = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = REL original_tag = REL any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_administration_registered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_adopted } has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_was_absent NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		ac_release_REL_select_owned_capital = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
			ac_econ_adopt_release_account = yes
			ac_pol_initialize = yes
			ac_release_REL_physical_registration = yes
			set_country_flag = ac_release_administration_registered
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_administration_registered
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = no }
		ac_release_REL_select_owned_capital = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_adopted ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
		ac_econ_adopt_release_account = yes
		ac_pol_initialize = yes
		set_country_flag = ac_release_administration_registered
		set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_administration_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } has_global_flag = ac_release_REL_maintenance_registered }
		ac_release_REL_prepare_maintenance = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SBK original_tag = SBK any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_administration_registered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_adopted } has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_was_absent NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		ac_release_SBK_select_owned_capital = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
			ac_econ_adopt_release_account = yes
			ac_pol_initialize = yes
			ac_release_SBK_physical_registration = yes
			set_country_flag = ac_release_administration_registered
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_administration_registered
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_transfer_done
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_entry_begun
		}

		limit = { tag = SBK original_tag = SBK any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = no }

	if = {

		ac_econ_adopt_release_account = yes
		ac_pol_initialize = yes
		set_country_flag = ac_release_administration_registered
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_administration_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SBK original_tag = SBK any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } has_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_maintenance_registered }
		ac_release_SBK_prepare_maintenance = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SER original_tag = SER any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_administration_registered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_adopted } has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_was_absent NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		ac_release_SER_select_owned_capital = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
			ac_econ_adopt_release_account = yes
			ac_pol_initialize = yes
			ac_release_SER_physical_registration = yes
			set_country_flag = ac_release_administration_registered
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_administration_registered
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = no }
		ac_release_SER_select_owned_capital = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_adopted ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
		ac_econ_adopt_release_account = yes
		ac_pol_initialize = yes
		set_country_flag = ac_release_administration_registered
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_administration_registered
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } has_global_flag = ac_release_SER_maintenance_registered }
		ac_release_SER_prepare_maintenance = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SHI original_tag = SHI any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_administration_registered } NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_adopted } has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_was_absent NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		ac_release_SHI_select_owned_capital = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ac_release_capital_selected ac_release_owned_capital = yes }
			ac_econ_adopt_release_account = yes
			ac_pol_initialize = yes
			ac_release_SHI_physical_registration = yes
			set_country_flag = ac_release_administration_registered
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_administration_registered
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SHI original_tag = SHI any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } ac_release_owned_capital = no }
		ac_release_SHI_select_owned_capital = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SHI original_tag = SHI any_owned_state = { NOT = { state = 171 } } has_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_adopted ac_release_owned_capital = yes }

	if = {

ac_release_record_annexation = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_registry_ready } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_adopted
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_annexed
		set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_transfer_done
		set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_entry_begun
		set_global_flag = ac_release_AWW_legacy_oob_done
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_awaiting_legacy_army
		ac_release_AWW_cancel_civilian = yes
		ac_release_AWW_cancel_military = yes
		ac_release_AWW_cancel_network = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_registry_ready } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_adopted
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_annexed
		set_global_flag = ac_release_REC_legacy_oob_done
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_awaiting_legacy_army
		ac_release_REC_cancel_civilian = yes
		ac_release_REC_cancel_military = yes
		ac_release_REC_cancel_network = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REL original_tag = REL }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_registry_ready } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_adopted
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_REL_annexed
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_awaiting_legacy_army
		ac_release_REL_cancel_civilian = yes
		ac_release_REL_cancel_military = yes
		ac_release_REL_cancel_network = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SBK original_tag = SBK }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_registry_ready } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_adopted
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_annexed
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_transfer_done
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SBK_entry_begun
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_awaiting_legacy_army
		ac_release_SBK_cancel_civilian = yes
		ac_release_SBK_cancel_military = yes
		ac_release_SBK_cancel_network = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SER original_tag = SER }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_registry_ready } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_adopted
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SER_annexed
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_awaiting_legacy_army
		ac_release_SER_cancel_civilian = yes
		ac_release_SER_cancel_military = yes
		ac_release_SER_cancel_network = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = SHI original_tag = SHI }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_registry_ready } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_adopted
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_SHI_annexed
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_awaiting_legacy_army

	if = {

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ac_release_registry_ready } }
			set_global_flag = ac_release_TER_adopted
		}
		set_global_flag = ac_release_TER_annexed
		clr_country_flag = ac_release_awaiting_legacy_army
		ac_release_TER_cancel_civilian = yes
		ac_release_TER_cancel_military = yes
		ac_release_TER_cancel_network = yes
	}
}
ac_release_interrupt_projects = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW has_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_active OR = { has_capitulated = yes NOT = { any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_AWW_civilian_target is_core_of = AWW is_fully_controlled_by = AWW } } } }
		ac_release_AWW_cancel_civilian = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW has_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_military_active OR = { has_capitulated = yes NOT = { any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_AWW_military_target is_core_of = AWW is_fully_controlled_by = AWW } } } }
		ac_release_AWW_cancel_military = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = AWW original_tag = AWW has_country_flag = ac_release_AWW_network_active OR = { has_capitulated = yes ac_release_AWW_network_target_valid = no } }
		ac_release_AWW_cancel_network = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC has_country_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_active OR = { has_capitulated = yes NOT = { any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_REC_civilian_target is_core_of = REC is_fully_controlled_by = REC } } } }
		ac_release_REC_cancel_civilian = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { tag = REC original_tag = REC has_country_flag = ac_release_REC_military_active OR = { has_capitulated = yes NOT = { any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = ac_release_REC_military_target is_core_of = REC is_fully_controlled_by = REC } } } }
		ac_release_REC_cancel_military = yes
	}
	if = {
